# B2 claim review items

Each item shows what B2 returns: extracted context, raw text and B2 metadata. Decide relevant / not_relevant / uncertain with the v2 criterion (evals/dev/README.md), then for every claim component mark complete / partial / none in the item_claim_judgments sheet (relevant needs at least one complete or partial; not_relevant and uncertain mean none everywhere). A composite claim is complete when the top-k units together cover all its components. One item can serve both queries of a matched pair.

## b2-0059 (new): fr:2025-15010, page - / -

- queries: dq04_en: sum of the column 1 duty rate and the reciprocal rate for Korea will be 15 percent
- claims: c1: For goods of Korea with a column 1 duty rate below 15 percent, the column 1 rate plus the additional rate under Executive Order 14257, as amended, is 15 percent. | c2: For goods of Korea with a column 1 duty rate of at least 15 percent, the additional rate under Executive Order 14257, as amended, is zero percent.

- context (extracted): source: fr:2025-15010 | document: Executive Order 14326 | title: Further Modifying the Reciprocal Tariff Rates | published: 2025-08-06

> European Union is determined by the good's current ad valorem (or ad valorem equivalent) rate of duty under column 1 (General) of the HTSUS ("Column 1 Duty Rate"). For a good of the European Union with a Column 1 Duty Rate that is less than 15 percent, the sum of its Column 1 Duty Rate and the additional ad valorem rate of duty pursuant to this order shall be 15 percent. For a good of the European Union with a Column 1 Duty Rate that is at least 15 percent, the additional ad valorem rate of duty pursuant to this order shall be zero. (d) Goods of any foreign trading partner that is not listed in Annex I to this
